### greet-zh

```

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届くこと」を保証し、UDPは速さを優先して保証しません。

TCPは届いたか・順番どおりか確認して再送するので、データが欠けず正しく届きます。UDPは確認も再送もしないぶん軽く速いので、動画配信やオンラインゲームのようなリアルタイム性が大事な場面で使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla, como ordenar o unir commits que nadie más tiene. Usa merge para integrar una rama que ya es pública o compartida, porque reescribir su historia rompe el trabajo de otros.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠有序的传输（保证送达、按序、不丢不重），UDP 是无连接、不保证可靠的传输（速度快、开销小，但可能丢包或乱序）。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to tidy its commits onto the latest base before you share it. Merge when the branch is already public or others have built on it, since rebasing would rewrite history they rely on.
```

如果只记得文件名的一部分，可以用关键词筛选：`bun test foo` 会跑路径里含 `foo` 的测试文件。要只跑某个 `test(...)` 用例，加 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）：`bun test foo -t "某个用例名"`。

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to the database before it touches the main data files, so that after a crash the database can replay the log and recover committed work. Because the log is only appended to sequentially, it also keeps writes fast and durable.
```

### rich-compare-en

```
--- step 1: user says: Put these side by side for me. Mac mini M4: $599, 16 GB, always plugged in, stays at home. MacBook Air M4: $999, 16 GB, runs on battery, comes with me. Which is the better always-on home for you?

### board-offer-go-en

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "bench"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with the nightly hawdb numbers, and it refreshes after each run. Night 3 came in at 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, no compaction stalls.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2091 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="wps">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">writes/s · p99 latency · compaction stalls</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <p class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>No nights yet. The first run will show here.</p>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var wpsEl = document.getElementById('wps');
  var subEl = document.getElementById('sub');
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');

  function fmtK(n) { return Math.round(n / 1000) + 'k'; }

  function render(runs) {
    runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs : [];
    list.innerHTML = '';
    if (!runs.length) {
      wpsEl.textContent = '—';
      empty.hidden = false;
      return;
    }
    empty.hidden = true;
    var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    wpsEl.textContent = fmtK(last.writesPerSec) + ' writes/s';
    subEl.textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99Ms + ' ms · compaction stalls ' +
      (last.stalls == null ? '—' : last.stalls);
    runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = 'Night ' + r.night;
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + r.p99Ms + ' ms · stalls ' + (r.stalls == null ? '—' : r.stalls);
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = fmtK(r.writesPerSec) + '/s';
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }

  if (window.ducter) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(function (d) {
      render(d && d.runs ? d.runs : d);
    });
    ducter.onChange(function (d) {
      render(d && d.runs ? d.runs : d);
    });
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":2,"writesPerSec":181000,"p99Ms":4,"stalls":null},{"night":3,"writesPerSec":182000,"p99Ms":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

Write whole values (a key's value is replaced), once a round. Then don't report_to_main for a round that only updated it: report only when some
  [also used tools] app

    state.days.push({ items: [] });
    state.cur = state.days.length - 1;
    pendingDel = null;
    save();
    render();
  };

  $('save').onclick = function () {
    var name = $('name').value.trim();
    var amount = parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if (!name || !isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) return;
    state.days[state.cur].items.push({ name: name, amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100 });
    $('name').value = '';
    $('amt').value = '';
    pendingDel = null;
    save();
    render();
  };

  function apply(v) {
    if (v && Array.isArray(v.days) && v.days.length) {
      state = { days: v.days, cur: Math.min(v.cur || 0, v.days.length - 1) };
    }
    render();
  }

  try {
    var got = ducter.get('trip');
    if (got && typeof got.then === 'function') got.then(apply); else apply(got);
    ducter.onChange(function () {
      var v = ducter.get('trip');
      if (v && typeof v.then === 'function') v.then(apply); else apply(v);
    });
  } catch (e) { render(); }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with the four PRs. Tap one when it lands and it's ticked off, and the count at the top updates.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1901 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">of 4 PRs</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <p class="k-from">Tap a row when it lands.</p>
</main>
<script>
var DEFAULT = ducter.get("default") || [];
var list = document.getElementById('list');
var count = document.getElementById('count');
var sub = document.getElementById('sub');

function render(items) {
  list.innerHTML = '';
  items.forEach(function (p) {
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'k-row';
    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(!!p.done));
    b.style.width = '100%';
    b.style.textAlign = 'left';
    b.style.opacity = p.done ? '0.5' : '1';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = (p.done ? '☑ ' : '☐ ') + p.repo + ' · ' + p.title;
    if (p.done) main.style.textDecoration = 'line-through';
    var sb = document.createElement('div');
    sb.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sb.textContent = p.waiting;
    var v = document.createElement('span');
    v.className = 'k-row-value';
    v.textContent = p.done ? 'landed' : 'open';
    b.appendChild(main);
    b.appendChild(sb);
    b.appendChild(v);
    b.onclick = function () {
      p.done = !p.done;
      ducter.set('prs', items);
      render(items);
    };
    list.appendChild(b);
  });
  var left = items.filter(function (p) { return !p.done; }).length;
  count.textContent = String(left);
  sub.textContent = 'of ' + items.length + ' PRs';
  ducter.summary(left + ' of ' + items.length + ' still waiting');
}

function load() {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('prs')).then(function (v) {
    render(Array.isArray(v) && v.length ? v : DEFAULT);
  });
}

};
ducter.onChange(render);
render();

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让它再补一条正好到期那一刻的测试，补完告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」。

要做的：在 TTL 测试里补一条用例，覆盖缓存项恰好到期那一刻（假时钟推进到刚好等于 TTL 的时刻）的行为。需要明确这一刻的结果：到期时值是否还能读到，按现有实现的语义来断言，并在报告里说明你采用的边界（是 <= 还是 < ）。

完成标准：新用例通过；整个 TTL 测试文件连跑 20 次全部通过；报告里给出测试输出和断言的边界说明。

不在范围内：不合并，不推送，不开 PR；不改动已有用例的断言语义；不改 Cache.values() 相关的代码。修改提交在你当前的分支上即可。
```
